BY MUNICIPAL COMMISSIONER
Jalgaon Municipal Corporation, Jalgaon
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949
No. SEC/MC-2025/CR.21/Desk-5/2025.--- In exercise of the power conferred by Section 5 (3)
of The Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949 (Bom. LIX of 1949) (hereinafter referred to as the
“said Act”) after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion received, in the
response to the Draft Notification No. RNI No. MAHBIL/13/2/2011-TC, dated 3rd September 2025
fixing for the Jalgaon Municipal Corporation. The number and the extent of the wards into which the
said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft Notification. The State
Government or it’s authorised officer, which the Approval of the State Election Commission,
Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, hereby makes the following Notification ;
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Jalgaon Municipal Corporation,
under Section 5 of the Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949 the Number of Councillors to be
elected and number of wards as follows :-
Total number of councillors - 75
Four Councillors wards - 18
Three Councillors wards - 01
Five Councillors wards - 00
Total wards - 19
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 5 (3) of the Maharashtra Municipal
Corporation Act, 1949, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the
wards into which the said Municipal Corporation area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this
Notificaton.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

BY MUNICIPAL COMMISSIONER
Dhule Municipal Corporation, Dhule
NOTIFICATION
Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949
No. RANIAA/ MANAPA-2025/PRA.KRA.20/KA-5/2025.---- In exercise of the powers conferred
by Section 5 (3) of The Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, 1949 (Bom. LIX of 1949) (hereinafter
referred to as the “said Act”) after carefully taking into consideration the objection and suggestion
received, in response to the Draft Notification No. RANIAA/MANAPA-2025/PRA.KRA.20/KA-5/2025,
dated 1st September 2025 fixing for the Dhule Municipal Corporation. The number and the extent of
the wards into which the said municipal area shall be divided is shown in the schedule to said Draft
Notification. The State Government or it’s authorised officer, with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer hereby makes the following Notification ;
Whereas, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State Election
Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer, as specified for Dhule Municipal Corporation,
Dhule under Section 5 of the Maharashtra Municipal Corporation Act, the Number of Councillors to be
elected and number of wards is as follows :-
1. Total number of councillors - 74
2. Four councillors wards - 17
3. Three councillors wards - 02
4. Five councillors wards - 00
5. Total wards - 19

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers under Section 5 (3) of the Maharashtra Municipal
Corporation Act, 1949, the State Government or it’s authorised officer with the Approval of the State
Election Commission, Maharashtra or it’s authorised officer hereby fix the number and extent of the
wards into which the said Municipal Corporation area shall be divided as shown in the schedule to this
Notification.
This Notification shall take effect for the purpose of the next General Election held immediately
following the date of this Notification.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Amalner Municipal Council, Amalner
Notification
No. Vashi-1/376/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development
Department, dated 10 th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming
elections of the Amalner Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra
Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025 ;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd
October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Amalner Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Amalner Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Amalner Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY COLLECTOR
Bhusawal Municipal Council
Notification
No. BMC/ELECTION-2025/72/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban
Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the
upcoming elections of the Bhusawal Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for Reservation Draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the Reservation Draw for the wards of
the Bhusawal Municipal Council was conducted and published on 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Bhusawal Municipal
Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik
Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Bhusawal Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

AYUSH PRASAD,
Collector, Jalgaon.
Jalgaon, 8th October 2025.
------------------------

BY COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Chopda Municipal Council
Notification
No. Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban
Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the
upcoming elections of the Chopda Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025 ;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd
October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
Chopda Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Chopda Municipal Council,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Chopda Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

AYUSH PRASAD
(I.A.S.),
Collector, Jalgaon.
Jalgaon, 8th October 2025.
-------------------------------

BY COLLECTOR
Chalisgaon Municipal Council, Chalisgaon
Notification
No. CMC/Election/953/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban
Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the
upcoming elections of the Chalisgaon Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd
October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Chalisgaon Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.
Therefore I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Chalisgaon Municipal
Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik
Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Chalisgaon Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.
Details of Total Seats
Sr. No. Category Total Number of Seats Reserved for

AYUSH PRASAD,
Collector, Jalgaon.
Jalgaon, 8th October 2025.
-------------------------------

BY COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Pachora Municipal Council
Notification
No. Ja.Kra./NaPa/Sankalan/9/E Office Number/2561849/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical
boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Pachora Municipal Council have been published
in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nasik Divisional Supplement,
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No. 10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No. 11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Pachora Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore I, Aayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of

reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Pachora Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Pachora Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

1 2 3 4 5

BY COLLECTOR
Dharangaon Municipal Council
Notification
Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for
the upcoming elections of the Dharangaon Municipal Council have been published in the Government
of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th
September 2025;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated

3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Dharangaon Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore I, Aayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Dharangaon Municipal
Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik
Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Dharangaon Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered..

(I.A.S.),
Collector, Jalgaon.
Jalgaon, 8th October 2025.

BY COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Pachora Municipal Council
Notification
No. NP/Sankalan/9/E-Office No./2554708/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for
the upcoming elections of the Pachora Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No. 10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No. 11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Pachora Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Pachora Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nasik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Pachora Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

old highway, westwards to Shivtirth, then
northwards up to the entrance of

westwards past Pataleshwar Mandir to the
house of Bapu Dhobi, and in front of that,
southwards past the house of Ganesh
Kautik Patil to the house of Kailas Girdhar
Mali (Indragiridhari Bhavan). From there,

eastwards to Maruti Mandir, circling around

in Rani Laxmibai Nagar, northwards behind

up to the house of Narayan Dhondu Patil in
Par Galli.

No. 11
heading east along the city limit to Sheetal 11B 11 B -- General.
Dairy, continuing on the city boundary up to
Raj Garden. From there, northwards along
the 24-meter D.P. Road up to Gat No. 204,
then along the Pungav Nala up to the
house of Kirit Jain. From there, along the
adjacent drain to the public toilet near the
Marimata Temple premises, then crossing
the Amalner Road up to Shri Dadaji
Provision, which is in front of the house of
Sunil Bhila Pardhi.

of Sunil Bhila Pardhi’s house on Amalner
Road), southwards to the house of Suresh
Babulal Pardhi, and further to the house of
Prakash Devaram Patil, which is behind it
towards the south. From there, via the
Saheli Beauty Parlour, eastwards to the
house of Liladhar Vishwanath Wagh in
Rani Laxmibai Nagar, and up to the Tower.
From the tower, southwards via the road
near Vijayanand Hotel up to the Highway,
then along the NES High School wall
compound and behind Vani Sir’s house,
further continuing along the wall of Sarthak
1993 residence, then past the house of
Vrushali Nitin Patil, and along the road in
front of the Sahaj Yoga Dhyan Kendra.
From there, straight southwards to the
house of Suresh Yogiraj Patil in DD Nagar
Part 2, and then along the city boundary to
Manasvi Beauty Parlour.
South:From Manasvi Beauty Parlour,
westwards along the city limits up to the
house of Eknath Yuvraj Mahajan, then
along the city boundary to the Open Space.

from Om Nagar up to Gat No. 171.

Mangal Karyalay on the Old Highway, 12B 12B General.

BY COLLECTOR
Faizpur Municipal Council, Faizpur
Notification
No. OWD/FMC/Election/797/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban
Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the
upcoming elections of the Faizpur Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025 ;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No. 10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No. 11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Faizpur Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Faizpur Municipal Council
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nasik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Faizpur Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

Jalgaon, 8th October 2025.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Bhadgaon Municipal Council, Bhadgaon
Notification

No. NP/Sankalan/9/E-office/2561849/2025.--Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban
Development Department dated 10 June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the
upcoming elections of the Bhadgaon Municipal Council / Nagarpanchayat have been published in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One – A, Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated
30th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3 October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
3 October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Bhadgaon Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Aayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10 June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation
for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Bhadgaon Municipal Council, as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One – A, Nasik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Bhadgaon Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Savda Municipal Council, Savda
Notification

No.NP/C/9/KV/E-office No.1/2561849/2025.--Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the
Urban Development Department dated 10 June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for
the upcoming elections of the Savda Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 03 October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03 October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Savda Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 08th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10 June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation
for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Savda Municipal Council , as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nasik Divisional Supplement,
dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Savda Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Yawal Municipal Council, Yawal

No. .--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban

upcoming elections of the Yawal Municipal Council have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Yawal Municipal Council was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Yawal Municipal Council as
notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Yawal Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025. Objections
or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Varangaon Municipal Council, Varangaon
Notification
No. Na/Pa/sankalan/9/E-office No. 2561849/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by
the Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of
wards for the upcoming elections of the Varangaon Municipal Council have been published in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd
October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Varangaon Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Varangaon Municipal
Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik
Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Varangaon Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Nashirabad Municipal Council, Nashirabad
Notification
No. Na/Pa/sankalan/9/E-office No. 2561849/2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by
the Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of
wards for the upcoming elections of the Nashirabad Municipal Council have been published in the
Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated 3rd
October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Nashirabad Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Nashirabad Municipal
Council as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik
Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Nashirabad Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Erandol Municipal Council, Erandol
General Election 2025
Notification of Draft Reservation
No. Election/37/2025-26.-- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development
Department, dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming
elections of the Erandol Municipal Council have been published in the Government of Maharashtra
Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nashik Divisional Supplement, Dated 30th September 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
03rd December 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the
wards of the Erandol Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.

Therefore, I, Aayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department, dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming General Elections in the election wards of the Erandol Municipal Council,
as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Erandol Municipal Council on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY COLLECTOR
Muktainagar Nagarpanchayat
Notification
No. PSN/KV/KARYA-01/1371//2025.--- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban
Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the
upcoming elections of the Muktainagar Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of
Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A – Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September
2025.
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No. 10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025, and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No. 11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Muktainagar Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on dated 8th October 2025.
Therefore I, Aayush Prasad, District Collector, Jalgaon in exercise of the powers vested in me
under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the
Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of
reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Muktainagar
Nagarpanchayat as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A –
Nasik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Muktainagar Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered..

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, JALGAON
Shendurni Nagarpanchayat, Shendurni
Notification
No. /2025.-- Whereas, as per the schedule issued by the Urban Development
Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical boundaries of wards for the upcoming
elections of the Shendurni Nagarpanchayat have been published in the Government of Maharashtra
Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025 ;
and whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No. RANIA/NP-
2025/Pr.No.10/Ka-6, dated 3rd October 2025 and Order No. RANIA/NP-2025/Pr.No.11/Ka-6, dated
3rd October 2025, the powers to publish a Draft Notification for reservation draw have been delegated.
Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for the wards of
the Shendurni Nagarpanchayat was conducted and published on 8th October 2025.
Therefore, I, Ayush Prasad, Jalgaon, in exercise of the powers vested in me under the above-
mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per the order of the Urban
Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft proposal of reservation for
the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Shendurni Nagarpanchayat, as notified in
the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement,
dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they should
submit them to the Chief Officer, Shendurni Nagarpanchayat on or before 14th October 2025.
Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.

BY DISTRICT COLLECTOR, NANDURBAR
Navapur Municipal Council
Draft Reservation Notification
No. COLNDB-18011(29)/122/NPP-2025/1/1763438/2025.- Whereas, as per the schedule
issued by the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, the final geographical
boundaries of wards for the upcoming elections of the Navapur Municipal Council have been
published in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part One-A–Nashik Divisional
Supplement, dated 30th September, 2025.
And whereas, as per the orders of the Hon. State Election Commission, Order No.
/-/../-, and Order No. /-/../-,
the powers to publish a draft notification for reservation draw have been
delegated. Accordingly, as per the schedule mentioned in the said orders, the reservation draw for
the wards of the Navapur Municipal Council was conducted and published on 8th October, 2025.
Therefore, I, Dr. Mittali Sethi, I.A.S, District Collector, Nandurbar, in exercise of the powers
vested in me under the above-mentioned orders of the Hon. State Election Commission and as per
the order of the Urban Development Department dated 10th June 2025, hereby publish the draft
proposal of reservation for the upcoming general elections in the election wards of the Navapur
Municipal Council, as notified in the Government of Maharashtra Gazette, Extraordinary Part
One-A, Nashik Divisional Supplement, dated 30th September 2025.
In accordance with this notification, if citizens have any objections or suggestions, they
should submit them to the Chief Officer, Navapur Municipal Council on or before 3:00 PM of
14th October 2025. Objections or suggestions received after this date will not be considered.
